## Changelog

> **NOTE (2026-07-27):** the client-side on-prem **agents live under `backend/agents/` and are
> now LOCAL-ONLY — `git rm --cached`'d + gitignored, so they are NOT on GitHub** (scale, camera,
> tally connector, watchdog, vehicle counter, the edge agent, the client SETUP-GUIDE + DPD docs +
> the built `.exe`s). They still exist on the build machine and are referenced throughout this doc,
> but `git`/CI no longer track them. Reversible: remove the `backend/agents/` line from `.gitignore`
> and `git add backend/agents`. The server product (`backend/app/` + `frontend/`) is unaffected —
> it never imports from `backend/agents/`.

| Date | Feature |
|---|---|
| 2026-08-01 | **Gate Vehicle Counter — swappable detector (two model families by JSON) + offline benchmark + Apache-2.0 YOLOX option (LOCAL agent, not in git/CI).** Accuracy work on the on-prem `backend/agents/vehicle_counter_agent.py` (gitignored — edit source, rebuild exe, hand-install). **Root cause of "sometimes counts, sometimes not"** proven from live logs: the camera points at an indoor **staircase**, so ~all vehicle counts are low-confidence noise (today: car 3/3 <40%, truck 1/1 <40%, motorcycle 16/18 <40%) — no software tuning fixes a mis-aimed camera; secondary factors are the 640px downscale on a 2560×1440 feed, the 0.2 conf floor, and dropped pushes on network blips. **Built (Tier-1 levers, all open-source, zero new deps):** the `Detector` is now **family-aware** — `_FAMILY_SPECS` + shared `_preprocess`/`_decode`/`_run` handle **yolov8** (÷255, RGB, class-only conf), **yolox** (raw 0-255, BGR, `obj×cls`), and **yolov5**; new config keys **`model_family`** (must match `model_path`) + **`imgsz`** (honoured only on a dynamic-axis export — the Detector reads the ONNX's native H/W and overrides `imgsz` for a fixed-size model so a mismatch can never crash). `letterbox` gained a `normalize` flag + cv2 `INTER_AREA` downscale (proper area-averaging vs the old nearest-neighbour). New **`--benchmark <folder>`** CLI runs every resolvable model over the SAME frames and prints per-image detections + counts at ≥25/40/50% + mean-conf + latency (pick the winner offline, no live double-count). **Exported + bundled `yolox_s.onnx`** (Apache-2.0, 35.9 MB, input `[1,3,640,640]` → output `[1,8400,85]`, decode baked in) beside `yolov8s/n.onnx`; PyInstaller spec bundles all three; export recipe + gotchas in `backend/agents/DPD-YOLOX-EXPORT.md`. **Verified on real gate frames:** synthetic decode math exact (v8 0.900, yolox obj×cls 0.600); on a real person frame yolov8 88.3% vs **yolox 86.2%** (family decode correct); benchmark showed **yolox ~20% faster (335 vs 415 ms), higher mean-conf (44% vs 29%), far less low-conf noise (6 vs 10 dets)**. **Two versions coexist in one build — switch by JSON** (`model_path`+`model_family`, restart); manhotra runs from source so no exe rebuild needed to try it. `yolov8` = AGPL-3.0, `yolox` = Apache-2.0 (only the weights carry the license; agent code + OpenCV/onnxruntime/NumPy are permissive). **Frozen EXE rebuilt** (`pyinstaller vehicle_counter_agent.spec` → `dist/vehicle_counter_agent.exe`, **167.8 MB**, now bundling all THREE models: yolov8n/yolov8s/yolox_s; spec `for _m in (...'yolox_s.onnx')`); verified inside the frozen build via `--benchmark` (both bundled models load + detect: person 88%/86%). Client package re-zipped (`dist/VehicleCounter-Client.zip`) + re-staged to `C:\weighbridge-agent\` (exe + updated `vehicle_counter.example.json` [model_family/imgsz + swap help] + `VEHICLE-COUNTER-DEPLOYMENT.md` §8 two-family/benchmark section + `Find-Cameras.ps1`). **Also — RTSP torn-frame ("cluttered picture") fix, round 2:** the earlier drain-burst still handed back garbled frames because (a) FFmpeg *conceals* a corrupt/reference-missing frame (returns `ok=True` with a smeared top + clean bottom band) so the burst couldn't reject it, and (b) the EZVIZ is on Wi-Fi streaming 1440p → the encoder/decoder desync tears frames. `_rtsp_frame` now (1) opens with `OPENCV_FFMPEG_CAPTURE_OPTIONS = rtsp_transport;tcp|fflags;discardcorrupt|err_detect;aggressive|reorder_queue_size;0` so FFmpeg **drops** corrupt frames instead of concealing them, and (2) grabs a burst of 8 and keeps the **sharpest** (highest Laplacian variance — a torn frame is mostly a low-detail grey smear, so it scores far below a clean frame), reconnecting to resync to a fresh keyframe if the whole burst is below a smear floor. Verified live against the real camera: **8/8 frames clean at full 2560×1440** (sharpness ~2940, consistent) vs the previous torn captures. ⚠ Underlying cause is the Wi-Fi link + 1440p bitrate — the software now reliably picks the clean frame, but the durable fixes are the camera's **sub-stream (`/102`)** or **wiring the camera / better Wi-Fi**. ⚠ Still re-aim the camera at the gate before it counts real traffic (this proof frame is the indoor staircase, not the gate). Not committed (agent is local-only — `backend/agents/` gitignored; `dist/*.exe` distributed, not committed). Remaining follow-ups offered: ROI-crop + temporal-confirmation + push-buffer (further Tier-1 accuracy). |
| 2026-07-31 | **Fleet Fuel — plant-tank diesel now deducts from Store Inventory out-of-the-box (fixes a silent per-tenant gap) (`origin/main`, live).** A `plant_tank` diesel fill only issued from store inventory when `app_settings.fuel_config.diesel_item_id` was set (`fuel.py::create_fuel_entry`, the `and cfg.get("diesel_item_id")` guard); a tenant that never opened **Fuel → Settings** recorded fills that **silently did NOT reduce stock** (hit `sss-stone-crusher`; `manhotra-consulting` worked only because its item was configured — classic "same code, per-tenant data" divergence). Three-layer fix: **(1)** live config fix for sss (pointed `fuel_config.diesel_item_id` at its existing "Diesel" store item). **(2)** `fuel.py::_resolve_diesel_item_id()` — when unconfigured, auto-resolves an **UNAMBIGUOUS** diesel item (exactly one active `inventory_items` whose `name ILIKE '%diesel%'`), **persists** it into `fuel_config`, and uses it; ambiguous (0 or >1) → None, no guess (fill still records). Explicit config always wins; company-scoped; inactive ignored; the config write is best-effort so it can never block a fill. **(3)** tenant provisioning (`multitenancy/router.py::_seed_tenant_data`, shared by both super-admin + platform create paths) now **seeds a "Diesel" store item** (`fuel`/`litre`, opening stock 0, idempotent via `WHERE NOT EXISTS`) and pre-points `fuel_config.diesel_item_id` at it — so a brand-new tenant deducts from **login 1** with zero setup. Verified real-DB: auto-resolve 7/7 (single→resolve+persist, ambiguous→None, inactive ignored, explicit wins, company-scoped) + seed 4/4 (idempotent single Diesel item, fuel_config points at it, resolver returns it). Pending follow-up (spawned chip): a non-blocking amber warning in Fuel → Settings + Record-Fill when no diesel item is configured (kept a warning, not a hard block — some tenants may not track diesel stock). |
| 2026-07-28 | **Day Book opening balance — every change is now audit-logged (fraud control) (`origin/main`, live).** The Day Book Opening B/F base (`app_settings.day_book_opening`, set via `PUT /reports/day-book-opening`, admin/accountant) directly drives the computed cash-in-hand / cash-in-bank, so a manual edit is a fraud surface. `put_day_book_opening` now reads the PREVIOUS value, then writes a central Audit Trail entry (`log_action`, entity_type `day_book_opening`) capturing **who** (user_id + username), **when** (timestamp), **from where** (client IP), and the **old → new** values. Surfaces in Accounts → Audit Trail (filter entity type = day_book_opening). Verified real-Postgres 10/10: create + update both logged, old cash 1000 → new 99999 captured, user/timestamp/IP present, malformed date still 400, empty payload safe. (`Request` added to the reports.py fastapi import; the actual table is `audit_log` singular.) |
| 2026-07-29 | **Platform → Telegram usage dashboard (platform_admin).** New tab in the Platform Admin console showing **Telegram messages sent per day across ALL tenants** with history. New `GET /api/v1/platform/telegram-stats?days=N` (`require_platform_role('platform_admin')`, `days` 1–365 default 30) fans out to every active tenant DB (`get_tenant_session(slug)`), counts `notification_log WHERE lower(channel)='telegram'` grouped by **IST day** + status, and aggregates into a **gap-filled daily series** (oldest→newest) + **per-tenant totals** + KPI roll-ups (`today` · `last7` · `sent`/`failed` over the window · tenants). A tenant DB that errors / lacks the table is skipped. **Frontend** `PlatformDashboard.tsx` gains a **Telegram** tab (platform_admin only): 7/30/90-day range toggle, 4 KPI cards, a Recharts stacked bar chart (Sent blue · Failed red) of messages/day, a per-tenant table, and CSV export — all in the console's dark theme. Verified against live data (sss-stone-crusher: 789 sent / 422 failed, correct IST per-day split); backend import OK; `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 + production build clean. |
| 2026-07-28 | **Accounting audit fixes — financial sanity guard (fat-finger / impossible / KG-vs-MT 1000× trap) (from the `manhotra-consulting` audit, #1+#2).** The audit found FINAL invoices the engine had computed faithfully but nothing sanity-checked: a **₹236-crore** Bill of Supply (590 MT × ₹40,00,000/MT), **53,788-MT** truck weights, and the **KG-vs-MT 1000× trap** (product `Sand-60mm` `unit=KG, rate=₹550` billed on a full-kg weighbridge quantity → ₹1.32 crore). New `services/sanity.py::check_line_sanity` normalises each line and **hard-blocks at `finalise_invoice`** (the single gate into the books/GST, BEFORE a number is assigned — the physical weighment + draft survive; the operator fixes the rate/weight/unit and re-finalises) when any of: a line **rate** > `max_rate_per_unit` (default ₹5,00,000), a line **amount** > `max_line_amount` (default ₹1 crore), a weight-unit **quantity** normalised to MT (KG÷1000·QUINTAL÷10·MT×1) > `max_qty_mt` (default 100), or the invoice **net weight** > 100 MT. The band is **generous** (no realistic stone-crusher / grain invoice trips it — a small mislabeled-KG line at ₹17k passes) and **tenant-configurable** via `app_settings.sanity_limits` (new `GET`/`PUT /api/v1/app-settings/sanity-limits`, admin PUT). The deposit auto-sweep's finalise is wrapped in try/except so it just skips a garbage draft. **Frontend:** ProductsPage shows an amber inline warning at the origin of the trap — a weight-unit product whose rate implies an absurd ₹/MT (e.g. *"₹550/KG = ₹5,50,000/MT — did you mean per MT?"*). Validated: unit + real-DB finalise 10/10 (blocks ₹236cr / ₹40-lakh-rate / 53,788-MT / KG-trap-₹1.32cr; passes realistic 24 MT × ₹550 and the small mislabeled-KG line; garbage stays a draft, never numbered); backend import OK; `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 + production build clean. |
| 2026-07-28 | **Accounting audit fixes — revision supersede + write-off invariant + invoice-number unique index (from the live `manhotra-consulting` financial audit).** Three real defects surfaced by a read-only integrity audit. **(H3) A finalised revision now SUPERSEDES its predecessor.** Previously `_finalize_revision_diff` only wrote a diff — the original stayed `status='final'`, and since every money query filters `status='final'`, a revised invoice's original **and** revision were both counted → **double-counted revenue/receivables** (audit found 3 such pairs, e.g. one billed ₹10,917.77 twice) plus **duplicate GST invoice numbers**. New `invoices.py::_supersede_prior_revisions` (called in `finalise_invoice`'s revision branch, before advance-apply) sets prior finalised chain members to **`status='superseded'`** (auto-excluded from balance / sales register / P&L / GSTR since they filter `='final'`) and releases their payments back to the party's advance pool; the append-only stock ledger is left intact (the original posted the one physical movement once). The 3 `!= 'cancelled'` money sites that would have leaked `superseded` back in (Customer-360 lifetime stats `parties.py`, token-register + `print_token` most-recent-invoice picks) now use `status.not_in(['cancelled','superseded'])`. **(H4) Write-off can no longer exceed a paid invoice.** `write_off_invoice` + `write_off_bulk` now compute the balance as **`grand_total − amount_paid − write_off_amount`** (recomputed from source, never the possibly-stale `amount_due`), so `amount_paid + write_off_amount` can never pass `grand_total` — closes the double-settlement that left one audited invoice both fully paid AND fully written off (phantom party credit + phantom bad-debt expense in P&L). **(Index) `ux_invoices_no_active`** replaces `ux_invoices_no_per_branch` (`ddl.py`): unique on `(company_id, COALESCE(branch_id,…), invoice_no) WHERE invoice_no IS NOT NULL AND status NOT IN ('cancelled','superseded')` — DB-level protection against duplicate live invoice numbers (the old index had failed to create on tenants with legacy dups; the new one excludes voided rows so it can). Validated real-DB rollback: **H3 8/8** (revision → `/Rv2` distinct number · original superseded · party balance = single count not doubled · no duplicate active numbers · history preserved), **H4 7/7** (fully-paid → blocked · partial caps at true remaining · stale `amount_due` can't be exploited · invariant `paid+writeoff≤grand`); index creates on a clean DB; 55/55 tally+accounting regression tests green; backend import OK. ⚠ **Legacy data NOT auto-healed:** the 3 pre-existing duplicate-number pairs + the 1 paid-and-written-off invoice on manhotra remain until a one-time cleanup is run (offered separately); the new index won't create on a tenant until its legacy dups are healed (per-statement DDL skips the failure). |
| 2026-07-28 | **Prepaid deposit now AUTO-CLEARS open bills + FINALISES drafts (committed `8b7fadc`, deploy pending confirmation).** Fixes the gap that a deposit was tracked as a separate "wallet": it netted into the party's overall `current_balance` but the specific outstanding bills stayed open and the surplus sat unallocated. Now, right after a deposit is recorded, `invoices.py::_sweep_party_deposit` sweeps the party's OPEN bills **oldest-first** and clears them with the deposit, **finalising draft bills as it goes**: a **draft** → `finalise_invoice` (assigns the GST number + all normal side-effects: stock post, IRN, Tally auto-sync, notification), which auto-consumes the advance against it (FIFO) — but a draft is finalised **only when the deposit can FULLY clear it** (never a zero-value draft, never a partial-finalise); a **final** with a balance → apply the advance to the remainder (partial OK). Stops as soon as the deposit is exhausted; the surplus stays as a prepaid advance. Wired into `create_receipt` (customer→sale) + `create_voucher` (supplier→purchase, non-expense; expense vouchers skipped). Reuses the existing `_apply_party_advances` FIFO + `party_advance_remaining`. **Gated by the existing `advance.auto_apply` app-setting (default ON)** — turn it OFF to keep pure wallets (no auto-finalise). Never raises — a sweep failure can't undo the recorded deposit. ⚠ **Live impact:** with the default ON, recording a customer receipt / supplier voucher will now finalise the party's fully-covered draft bills (generating numbers/IRN/Tally from a deposit) — intended per the "it should finalize invoice also" requirement; disable `advance.auto_apply` for manual control. Validated real-DB rollback 13/13 (draft 1000 + deposit 2000 → draft finalised + cleared, 1000 advance, net −1000 · existing final 1500 + 1500 → cleared, 0 advance · auto_apply OFF → untouched, full amount stays advance · partial deposit 500 vs 1000 draft → draft left a draft, 500 advance); 46/46 Tally tests green; backend import OK. |
| 2026-07-28 | **Finance leakage controls — maker-checker (4-eyes) approvals + daily operator cash-count reminder (`origin/main`, live).** Two per-tenant governance controls. **(1) Maker-checker (blocking true 4-eyes), default OFF → fully inert until an admin enables it (Approvals page):** new `approval_requests` table (`ApprovalRequest` model + DDL) + `services/approvals.py` engine + `routers/approvals.py` (`GET /api/v1/approvals`, `/pending-count`, `POST /{id}/approve`, `/{id}/reject`, `GET/PUT /config`). Four sensitive money actions are gated **at the top, after their own validation, right before the first mutation** — `POST /invoices/{id}/write-off`, `POST /invoices/write-off-bulk`, `POST /invoices/{id}/cancel` (invoices.py) + `PUT /reports/day-book-opening` (reports.py). When the toggle is ON they PARK the request and return **HTTP 202 `{status:'pending_approval'}`** instead of executing; a **different** admin approves → `apply_approved` replays the real endpoint with `_bypass_approval=True` **as the original maker** (mutation attributed to the maker, checker recorded on the row); reject discards. **Self-approval is blocked (403)** — true 4-eyes. Re-validation runs at approval time (state may have changed since submit). Every submit/approve/reject is audit-logged; `approval_requested`/`approval_decided` Telegram templates seeded (delivered to recipients subscribed to those events). **Frontend:** `ApprovalsPage` (admin gear dropdown, `/approvals` in `ADMIN_ROUTES`) — ON/OFF Switch + pending queue (Approve/Reject, own-request buttons disabled) + decisions history; new `lib/approvalGate.ts::wasSubmittedForApproval(res)` handles the 202 at all 4 call-sites (InvoicesPage cancel + write-off, CustomerProfilePage bulk write-off, DayBookPage opening) — shows an info toast + stops the success path; sidebar item (ShieldCheck) + en/hi i18n `sidebar.approvals`. Returning a `JSONResponse` from a `response_model` route skips serialization, so the gate is safe on the typed endpoints. **(2) Operator cash-count reminder (non-blocking owner alert), default ON:** `reports.py::build_cash_count_reminder_context` finds operators who COLLECTED cash today but did NOT record a drawer count (`operator_cash_counts.counted_cash IS NULL`); fired from the existing daily owner-digest loop (`main.py`, at `owner_digest.time`), gated by `app_settings.operator_cash_count.enabled` (default ON), **sent only when someone is missing a count**, via a seeded `operator_cash_count_missing` Telegram template (owner subscribes on /notifications, same pattern as `eod_summary`). **Validated:** 18/18 (day-book replay) + 9/9 (write-off replay) real-DB rollback assertions — submit parks (target UNCHANGED) · self-approve 403 · different-admin approve APPLIES · reject discards · bypass applies directly; backend import OK; frontend `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 + production build clean. Deploys **inert** (maker-checker OFF for every tenant → zero behaviour change; `approval_requests` table + templates auto-create on the next backend restart per tenant). **Live-verified:** pushed to `main` (`7bc3dea..1e71cf4`) → CI deploy green; health 200 + `GET /api/v1/approvals/config` returns 401 (router deployed + auth-gated) on the tenant subdomain. Also shipped: `docs/USER-MANUAL-ACCOUNTANT.md` (plain-language accountant guide). |
| 2026-07-28 | **Notification bug — Telegram daily-summary showed raw `<b>`/`<code>` tags in the trip lines (`origin/main`, live).** Root-caused, not guessed: the notification renderer (`service.py::render_template`) uses a Jinja `Environment(autoescape=select_autoescape(["html"]))`, and `render_template` renders via `from_string` — Jinja's `default_for_string=True` means **autoescape is ON for string templates**. The `anpr_daily_summary` template's OWN literal tags (the bold title) pass through and render, but the injected **`{{ trip_list }}`** — which `_build_daily_summary_context` pre-builds with `<b>…</b>`/`<code>…</code>` — got HTML-escaped to `&lt;b&gt;`, and since the Telegram sender uses `parse_mode=HTML`, `&lt;` displayed as a literal `<`. So only the per-trip lines (from the variable) showed raw tags; the title (literal) rendered bold — the diagnostic tell. **Fix (context-level, version-independent):** `fmt_trip` now `html.escape`s the free-text data (plate, gate-pass, product) while keeping the structural tags, and the context returns `trip_list` as `markupsafe.Markup(...)` so autoescape leaves the intentional tags intact. Fixes BOTH the old ("Gate Camera Report") and v3 ("Daily Weighment Summary") templates since it's fixed in the context builder, not the stored template body. Verified through the REAL `render_template` (autoescape on): Markup trip_list → real `<b>RJ..</b>`/`<code>` preserved, zero `&lt;`; a plain-str control still escapes (proves the mechanism); a hostile plate `AB<script>` → neutralised to `AB&lt;script&gt;`. Backend import OK. |
| 2026-07-27 | **Gate vehicle counting — optional human/person detection, JSON-toggled per site (`origin/main`, live).** The bundled YOLOv8n already detects `person` (COCO id 0), but the agent filtered to vehicles only via a hardcoded map — so counting people needed a rebuild. Made it **config-controlled**: the agent's class map is now `COCO_CLASSES = {0:person,1:bicycle,2:car,3:motorcycle,5:bus,7:truck}` and `classes` in `vehicle_counter.json` selects which to count — **add `"person"` to count people, no rebuild** (one-time exe rebuild ships the person-aware map; toggling is JSON thereafter). Backend: `ALLOWED_CLASSES` gained `person`; `/vehicle-count/counts` keeps people **separate** — `totals` + `reconciliation.camera_entries` stay **vehicles-only** (so counting people never skews "Vehicles IN" or the gate-pass reconciliation) and a new `people:{entries,exits}` field carries the person tally. Frontend: `person` label + User icon + a conditional **"People detected IN/OUT"** banner (shown only when >0) + `person` in the events filter. Default `classes` stays vehicles-only → existing sites unchanged. Caveat (documented): counts are **presence-based** (one per appearance after the frame clears), so `person` is a people-*activity* signal, not a crowd headcount. Verified: agent py_compile + `COCO_CLASSES` has person; backend import + real-Postgres counts test (2 truck+1 car+2 person in, 1 person out → vehicles_in=3, people_in=2, people_out=1, reconciliation=3 vehicles-only); `tsc` 0; exe rebuilt (74 MB, boots) + copied to the client bundle. |
| 2026-07-27 | **Autonomous gate vehicle counting — tenant-agnostic ingest gate + bundled-model exe (`origin/main`, live).** Makes the counter a **single universal `.exe`** that can be deployed to any client, with the **platform admin's `vehicle_count` module toggle as the sole on/off control** ("who wants it vs who doesn't"). The gap: the agent ingest (`POST /vehicle-count/event`) is agent-key (no JWT), so the middleware module-gate never ran for it — an installed exe would push data even for a tenant with the module OFF. Fixed: `ingest_vehicle_event` (multi-tenant branch) now checks `middleware._get_tenant_modules(tenant_slug)['vehicle_count']` right after agent-key auth and returns **403 "not enabled for this tenant"** when off, so an installed agent on a non-subscribed tenant is **inert** until the platform admin flips the module on (≤5 min module-cache propagation). Nothing baked into the exe — `tenant_slug`+`agent_key` come from `vehicle_counter.json` at runtime; the example config was genericized (placeholders, no tenant). The `.exe` was **rebuilt with the model bundled** (`yolov8n.onnx` exported via ultralytics + placed next to the spec → PyInstaller bundles it) so a client deployment is just **exe + config** (the external-model override still works for upgrades). Verified: backend import + `_get_tenant_modules` async + `vehicle_count` default False. |
| 2026-07-27 | **Autonomous gate vehicle counting — Phase 3: edge YOLO agent (single .exe) + snapshot retention (`origin/main`, live).** The counting brain. New **standalone** `backend/agents/vehicle_counter_agent.py` (mirrors `watchdog_agent`/`tally_connector` — NOT a change to the live scale/camera agents): grabs a frame from the entry+exit gate cameras every ~1.5 s, runs **YOLOv8n (ONNX, CPU, onnxruntime)**, maps COCO→`truck/car/motorcycle/bus`, **de-dups** (rising-edge + `cooldown`/`min_absent` so a lingering vehicle counts once, a new one after a gap counts again), sets **direction = which camera fired**, and POSTs each count + snapshot to `/api/v1/vehicle-count/event` (agent-key auth + `tenant_slug`, reusing the SAME key as `camera_config.json`; apex→subdomain routing; onnxruntime lazy-imported so `--setup`/import work without it). CLI `--setup`/`--test`/`--install`(NSSM `WeighbridgeVehicleCounter`)/`--uninstall`; local status UI on :9011. **Ships as a single frozen `.exe`** — `vehicle_counter_agent.spec` (PyInstaller, `collect_all('onnxruntime')` + bundles `yolov8n.onnx`; external model still wins at runtime) + `vehicle_counter_requirements.txt` + `vehicle_counter.example.json` + `DPD-VEHICLE-COUNTER.md` runbook (get model → `pyinstaller vehicle_counter_agent.spec` → `dist/vehicle_counter_agent.exe`, no Python on the client). **Retention (cloud):** `gate_count.purge_old_vehicle_events` now also deletes snapshot day-dirs >30 d (not just rows), wired into the existing daily `_owner_digest_loop` purge block per tenant. Verified: agent `py_compile` + import (onnxruntime truly lazy) + 16/16 pure-logic units (apex routing · COCO map · NMS · letterbox · de-dup lingering=1/gap=2/cooldown-block/independent-classes · config merge); backend import + real-Postgres retention purge (40-day row deleted, recent kept). **The `.exe` was built + validated** (`backend/agents/dist/vehicle_counter_agent.exe`, 45 MB, PyInstaller `--onefile`): frozen boot OK, argparse intact, **onnxruntime imports + runs inside the frozen exe** (proven by a dummy-model load rejected with `INVALID_PROTOBUF`, i.e. the runtime executed), apex→subdomain routing OK. Built WITHOUT bundling the model (that needs the heavy torch/ultralytics export toolchain) — the exe loads an **external `yolov8n.onnx`** at runtime (`resolve_model_path` checks next-to-exe first), so a client deployment folder = **exe + `yolov8n.onnx` (~12 MB, one-time `yolo export model=yolov8n.pt format=onnx`) + `vehicle_counter.json`**; drop-in a new model without a rebuild. `dist/*.exe` is gitignored (distributed, not committed) like the other agent exes. **Next: Phase 4 — enable `vehicle_count` for sss + install the exe on the gate PC + verify counts/reconciliation.** |

| 2026-07-26 | **Finance controls remediation — Phase 3 (final): manual stock-adjusted COGS (F3) + pass-through documentation (F6) — roadmap COMPLETE (`origin/main` e09edb4, live).** Last of the 3-phase finance-module controls roadmap; both forks per the client's accounting decisions. **F3 (stock-adjusted COGS — MANUAL opening/closing inputs, client's chosen approach):** the stock tables carry **quantity only (no cost basis)**, so an auto-valuation engine would misstate taxable profit — instead the accountant enters opening & closing stock **VALUES (₹, the CA's figures)** and COGS becomes goods ACTUALLY sold: **COGS = opening + purchases − closing**. `GET /reports/profit-loss` gains optional `opening_stock`/`closing_stock` query params — when supplied, a period-level stock-adjustment of `(opening − closing)` is added to raw purchases (surfaced in the summary as `purchases`, `opening_stock`, `closing_stock`, `stock_adjustment`, `stock_adjusted`); when omitted, COGS = purchases-in-period (**unchanged, non-breaking**). New `GET/PUT /reports/stock-valuation` (`app_settings.stock_valuation`; PUT admin/accountant) persists the figures so the P&L tab pre-fills them. Frontend `ReportsPage` P&L tab: Opening/Closing ₹ inputs + "Save stock values" + a goods-sold COGS breakdown (Opening → Purchases → Closing → COGS) shown only when adjusted. **F6 (pass-through charges — keep net-zero + document, client's chosen approach):** freight, vehicle rent and royalty are billed at cost and stay excluded from both revenue and expense (net-zero, no calc change) — surfaced as a caveat in the P&L `notes[]` and rendered as footnotes on the report so the treatment is explicit for the CA. Validated on real Postgres (rolled back), 12/12: adjusted COGS = purchases + (opening − closing); gross = revenue − effective COGS; closing>opening lifts gross by exactly the delta; no-params path leaves COGS = purchases; notes[] ≥ 2; stock-valuation PUT→GET round-trips. Backend import OK; `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0. **Roadmap COMPLETE — all 7 findings closed across Phases 1-3 (F1 Day Book cash/accrual · F2 overhead expense capture · F3 stock-adjusted COGS · F4 cancel reverses advances · F5 ledger opening b/f · F6 pass-through documentation · F7 P&L note guard).** |
| 2026-07-26 | **Finance controls remediation — Phase 2: cancel reverses advances (F4) · ledger opening b/f (F5) · P&L note guard (F7) (`origin/main` 6f2405c, live).** Second of a 3-phase roadmap from the finance-module controls review. **F4 (cancel reverses payment/advance allocations):** `cancel_invoice` now DELETEs the invoice's `InvoicePayment` links and resets `amount_paid`/`amount_due`=0 (`payment_status='unpaid'`) BEFORE the balance recompute — so an advance auto-applied at finalise is RETURNED to the party's unallocated pool instead of staying stranded on a dead invoice (previously the party was permanently under-credited by the applied amount). **F5 (party ledger opening balance brought-forward):** `party_ledger` now fetches the party's FULL history (no lower date bound, `<= to_date` only) and folds every row dated before the window into an **opening balance b/f**, emitting only in-window rows — so the running balance closes at exactly `Party.current_balance` regardless of the date window / FY boundary. Also excludes direct-expense vouchers (`expense_category IS NOT NULL`) from the party ledger (they're P&L overheads, not party payables — consistent with `balances.py`). **F7 (P&L note guard, latent/future-proofing):** the credit/debit-note revenue-netting query in `profit_loss` now excludes any note whose `reference_invoice_id` points to a PURCHASE invoice (a purchase-side note belongs against COGS, not revenue) via a `~exists()` subquery; unreferenced notes stay sale-side (today's only path). Validated on real Postgres (rolled back), 11/11: F4 pre-cancel −2000 (advance 5000 − 3000 applied) → cancel deletes IP links + resets paid → post-cancel −5000 (full advance back); F5 opening b/f 1000, in-window entries 2, closing 2500 == `recompute_party_balance`; F7 sale-note netted (1500) / purchase-note excluded. Backend import OK. **Phase 3 remaining: F3 (stock-adjusted COGS — needs a costing-method decision + CA note) + F6 (pass-through freight/rent/royalty treatment policy).** |

| 2026-07-26 | **Notification templates now seed for ALL tenants at startup — SaaS tenants stopped getting new event alerts (root-caused on live data).** Reported: "Stone inventory telegram notifications are not coming" (`sss-stone-crusher`). **Proven, not assumed** — queried the live tenant DB (`wb_sss_stone_crusher`): telegram config **enabled + token present**, both recipients subscribed to **`["*"]`** (all events), yet `notification_templates` held **39 rows across only 18 event types** with EVERY event added this session **missing** — `inventory_transaction`, `gate_pass_created`, `gate_pass_exit`, `invoice_write_off`, `diesel_transaction`, `payment_made`, `worker_payment`. `send_notification` loads templates for the event first and **returns early when none exist** (no send, no log row) — so the alert silently never fired. **Root cause:** the startup `seed_default_templates` call sat **inside the single-tenant `else` branch** and only touched the **default DB** ("Per-tenant template seeding happens on-demand via the Notifications page") — so in multi-tenant/SaaS mode template seeding **never ran at startup at all**, and a tenant seeded once only ever received event types that existed *at that moment*; anything added later stayed missing until an admin happened to open the Notifications page on that tenant. **Fix (`main.py`):** `seed_default_templates` now runs for **every active tenant inside the startup DDL loop** (idempotent — `seed_default_templates` inserts only absent `(event_type, channel)` pairs), so a newly-added event auto-seeds on the next deploy across ALL tenants. Named recipients are still **never** auto-seeded (each tenant adds its own → alerts go only to that tenant's own bot/chats). The lazy on-page seeding path is untouched (harmless belt-and-suspenders). Verified: backend import OK; live re-query of `wb_sss_stone_crusher` after the deploy-restart confirms the 7 missing templates now present. No frontend/schema change. |

Added a single `ist` Jinja filter in `app/utils/pdf_generator.py` — converts a UTC/naive **datetime** to `Asia/Kolkata` (UTC+5:30); a pure `date` value passes through **untouched** (a calendar date has no timezone and must not shift — `isinstance datetime` is checked before `date`). Applied to every datetime field: **gate_pass_a4** (entry_time, exit_time) · **token_a4** (the shared `format_dt` macro → completed/first/second-weight + printed-at) · **token_thermal** (DATE/TIME + RECORDED/IN/OUT) · **token_report_a4** (created_at fallback) · **invoice** (sales+purchase; irn_ack_date) · **delivery_challan** (ewb_valid_till). Date-only fields (`token_date`, `invoice_date`, `due_date`, `challan_date`) deliberately left as-is. On-screen displays were already IST (frontend `toLocaleString` uses the viewer's browser tz; the Gate-Pass/Token register report pages already pin `Asia/Kolkata`) — the PDFs were the only UTC gap. Verified: filter unit tests (aware-UTC + naive-UTC → +5:30, pure date unchanged, None safe); all 6 templates compile; end-to-end render of gate pass (entry 09:00 / exit 14:45 IST), token_a4 (completed 14:45 IST) and invoice (irn_ack 09:00 IST) from a known UTC input; live health 200 post-deploy. |
| 2026-07-25 | **Device Health — scale + camera heartbeat monitor + Telegram down-alerts (new feature module `device_health`, default ON).** The owner is alerted on Telegram when any camera or scale stays down past a configurable threshold, with a live Device Health dashboard. **Deliberately non-invasive — NO change to the scale or camera agents.** **Architecture:** a NEW standalone `backend/agents/watchdog_agent.py` runs on each plant PC (one per PC, matching the 2-PC layout: weighbridge + gate), probes the local scale (via the scale agent's `/status`) and each camera (fetches a snapshot), and POSTs a per-device heartbeat to the cloud; the SERVER stores per-device health, powers the dashboard, and a background loop fires the alert. **Backend:** new `device_health` table (`ddl.py` — one row per (company, device_key), upserted per heartbeat; `last_ok_at` frozen while down so "down for N min" is measurable); new `routers/monitor.py` — `POST /api/v1/monitor/heartbeat` (agent-key auth, no JWT — like the scale/tally agent routes; the module-gate lives inside the JWT-context branch so the agent heartbeat is never gated), `GET /monitor/health` (JWT dashboard feed with effective status **online** / **offline** [fresh heartbeat but device failing] / **stale** [no heartbeat → watchdog/PC offline]), `GET/PUT /monitor/config` (admin; `app_settings.device_health`, defaults `{enabled:true, down_threshold_min:5, stale_min:3}`). New `_device_health_loop` in `main.py` — per-tenant scan every 60 s; fires `device_down` once when a device is down past `down_threshold_min` (device-failure OR stale), and `device_recovered` once on recovery; supervised + cancelled on shutdown. Two Telegram templates (`device_down` / `device_recovered`) seeded in `seed_default_templates`; owner subscribes via the /notifications Recipients tab. **Feature-module wired (default True):** `auth.py DEFAULT_MODULES` + `middleware.py _ROUTE_TO_MODULE` (`/api/v1/monitor`) + `PlatformDashboard MODULE_META` + `Sidebar HUB_MODULES`. **Agent** (single deployable file, mirrors `scale_agent`/`tally_connector`): probes `scale`/`camera`/`agent` device types, apex→subdomain push routing, local status UI, `--setup`/`--test`/`--install` (NSSM `WeighbridgeWatchdogAgent`)/`--uninstall`; the alert threshold lives on the SERVER (Settings), not the agent — it only reports ok/down every `push_interval_sec`; uses the SAME `agent_key` already in the PC's `scale_config.json`/`camera_config.json`. **Frontend:** `DeviceHealthPage` (`/device-health`, Operations section) — KPI strip + live device cards (online/down/no-signal) auto-refreshing every 15 s; Settings → **Device Health** config tab; RBAC catalogue + sidebar + route + types + en/hi i18n. Verified: real-Postgres SQL logic 11/11 (upsert freeze · down/stale/recover decisions · feed mapping); in-process HTTP-handler e2e 12/12 (heartbeat → DB → dashboard → config round-trip); agent py_compile + helper units 11/11; backend import + templates + loop wiring OK; tsc 0 + production build clean. Also fixed alongside: **invoice Est. Total** now includes freight + vehicle rent in both create/edit dialogs (the Vehicle Rent field loaded fine, but the preview summed line items only, so the rent never showed in the total). |

Also hardened the edge `_company_and_fy` to anchor on the ACTIVE financial year and take its company (robust even if the mirror ever carried a stray second company row). **Verified end-to-end, no network, two real databases in-process:** masters round-trip (Postgres→serialize→SQLite→ORM read, types clean incl. Decimal rates; 64 parties/61 products/58 vehicles); weight-idempotency (real Postgres: replay = same token, NO second invoice, one ledger row each); full edge→cloud (offline capture of token+first+second weight via the edge routes → `replay-one` applies once, COMPLETED, `origin='edge'`, one auto-invoice → second drain is a no-op, still one invoice). | 2026-07-19 | **P0: offline-saved weighments were being silently DELETED — fixed (first step of the offline-ops work).** Root-caused, not assumed. Since the offline queue shipped (16 Jun 2026) every token saved during an outage was destroyed on reconnect, *after* the operator was shown *"Saved offline — will sync when the connection returns."* **Two independent causes, one fatal rule:** (a) `TokenPageV1` called `enqueueToken('/tokens', …)` while `services/api.ts` sets `baseURL:'/'`, so replay POSTed `/tokens` — a route that does not exist (the router is `/api/v1/tokens`) → **404**; (b) the queue lives in `localStorage` but the JWT lives in `sessionStorage`, so after a browser restart replay had no auth → **401**. Both are 4xx, and `offlineQueue.ts:71-77` treated *any* 4xx as "permanent failure — drop it". The rule was right in spirit ("a malformed token can never succeed on retry") and catastrophically wrong in blast radius. **Fixes:** endpoint corrected at the call site **and** legacy items migrated forward on read (`normalizeEndpoint`) so tokens already stranded on operators' machines are **rescued**, not lost. The drop rule is replaced by a documented **safety invariant — a queued item is NEVER deleted because the server rejected it**: 401/403 → `needs_auth` (pause + resume after login); 409/422/other 4xx → `needs_review` (park + **halt**, preserving order because the duplicate-active-token guard is order-sensitive); network/5xx → retry with `attempts`/`last_error`. Only a confirmed 2xx auto-removes; `discardItem()` is the deliberate human escape hatch. Flushing is skipped entirely with no session, killing the boot-time 401 storm. **Timeout:** applied to token create + replay (10 s) but deliberately **NOT global** — `/backup/create` (pg_dump), `/backup/restore`, imports and `/reports/anomalies` legitimately exceed it and a blanket timeout would regress them. **`OfflineIndicator`** now probes `/api/v1/health` instead of trusting `navigator.onLine` (which reports "online" on a dead uplink — the tenant's actual failure mode) and surfaces `needs_review` so parked weighments can't go unnoticed. **DDL:** added the document-number unique indexes that never existed — `invoice_no`/`token_no` were guarded only by a row-locked sequence and a racy read-then-write probe (`tokens.py::_next_token_no`); they are the prerequisite for replay dedupe. **Scoped per branch deliberately:** `_next_invoice_no` keeps a separate sequence per `branch_id` but does **not** vary the prefix, so two branches legitimately both mint `INV/25-26/0001` today — a company-wide index would break the second branch's finalise. ⚠️ **That cross-branch duplicate is a pre-existing GST concern** (Rule 46(b) wants a unique series) — fix is to give each branch its own prefix. Verified: 16/16 assertions against the **real** `offlineQueue` module (esbuild-bundled, api stubbed) covering legacy rescue · 404/401/409 retention · ordering halt · post-login resume; real-Postgres rollback test proving both indexes create, the `COALESCE` guard constrains the default branch (Postgres treats NULLs as distinct, so a naive index would not), cross-branch numbers still allowed, drafts unconstrained, 0 legacy duplicates; tsc 0; production build clean. Full offline-ops design (SQLite edge agent, leased number series, intent-replay sync) in `~/.claude/plans/linear-splashing-map.md`. |

On create the kiosk converts the quantity to canonical CFT and posts `volume_cft` **+ `billing_unit`**, so the volume token bills at that unit's rate; the voice confirmation now speaks the chosen unit. The **weighbridge** path also now posts `billing_unit = weightUnit().code` (MT, or Qtl for maize) for parity with the New Trip form. `canProceed`/"still missing" gained a **Quantity** check for volume mode. No backend change (`/tokens/volume` + `/tokens` already accept `billing_unit`; `token_quantity`/`resolve_rate` handle the unit). Verified: `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 errors. |
| 2026-07-11 | **New Trip form — field reorder + Vehicle Type removed.** Operator-flow tweak to `TokenPageV1` `CreateTokenForm`: **Material** now sits **immediately after the vehicle number** (fastest-entry order), **Units** moved to **right after the Measurement Method** (so the operator picks method → unit → quantity in sequence), and **Agent (broker)** moved to **after the Gate Pass** block. The manual **Vehicle Type** picker was **removed** from the form — `tokens.vehicle_type` is still auto-filled from the vehicle master on vehicle-select and still submitted + shown in the token list, so no data is lost; the now-unused `vehicleTypes` state + its `/app-settings/vehicle-types` fetch were dropped. No backend or schema change. Verified: `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 errors. |
| 2026-07-11 | **New Trip form — "Units" picker driven by the measurement method.** Follow-up to the per-unit-rates feature, sharpening the weighbridge operator's unit choice (`TokenPageV1` `CreateTokenForm`). The old free-form **"Bill in"** dropdown (Auto / MT / QUINTAL / KG + volume units) is relabelled **"Units"** and now split by measurement method: **Weighbridge → the tenant's weight unit is auto-selected and locked** (shown as a read-only pill "MT (auto — weighbridge)", or **Qtl** for a maize tenant via the shared `weightUnit()` helper — so no maize regression); **Volume → a 3-option dropdown of Cubic Feet (CFT) · Cubic Meter (CBM) · Brass**, operator types the quantity in the chosen unit, or taps a tyre tile to auto-fill it (still overwritable). Volume conversion happens client-side to the canonical CFT before submit (`1 CBM = 35.3147 CFT`, `1 Brass = 100 CFT`) so the stored `volume_cft` + `billing_unit` feed the existing backend `token_quantity()` unchanged; switching the volume unit converts the entered quantity in place (600 CFT ⇄ 6 Brass ⇄ 16.99 CBM). Tyre-tile subtitles, the quantity label/placeholder/suffix, and the live weight-preview all now render in the selected unit. `resetForm` seeds `billing_unit` from the active method (weight-unit for weighbridge, CFT for volume) so a post-create reset never submits a blank unit. No backend change — `token_quantity`/`resolve_rate`/`validate_billing_unit` already handle the weight/volume split (weighbridge is a weight unit → volume-unit block never trips). Verified: `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 errors. |
| 2026-07-11 | **Sales Partner / Agents dashboards — date-filterable, daily/weekly/monthly trends + payouts + rename.** The agent report card became a proper dashboard, and the master became an overview. **Rename:** everywhere the UI said "Agents" now reads **"Sales Partner / Agents"** (sidebar `sidebar.agents` → "Sales Partners" / hi "सेल्स पार्टनर", page headers, dialog titles, "New Partner") — routes/API stay `/agents` (internal). **Backend:** new `GET /api/v1/agents/trend?date_from&date_to&granularity=day|week|month&agent_id?` — commission **earned** (by invoice date) vs **paid** (by payout date), Python-bucketed + gap-filled over the range, `agent_id` omitted = all partners combined; returns `{granularity, series:[{period,label,earned,paid,invoice_count}], totals}`. Helpers `_period_key`/`_period_label`/`_iter_periods` (week = Monday; guard-capped at 2000 buckets). **Frontend:** `AgentReportPage` (`/agents/:id`) rebuilt as a dashboard — date presets (This month / Last 30 / Last 90 / This FY) + custom range + **Day/Week/Month toggle**, gradient KPI cards (Due all-time · Earned/Paid/Bills in-range), a **recharts** earned-vs-paid bar chart, invoice drilldown, payouts list. `AgentsPage` overview gains the same filter + an all-partners earned-vs-paid trend chart + a **"Top partners by due"** horizontal bar (leaderboard) + a per-row **Record payout** action. New shared `components/AgentPayoutDialog.tsx` (used by both). Design note: "Due" is always **all-time** (true outstanding) while Earned/Paid follow the selected range — avoids the range-vs-cumulative trap. Verified: bucketing unit tests (day/week/month keys + iter counts + labels) + end-to-end trend aggregation on a real tenant DB (month earned 300/paid 50/count 2; 31 daily buckets correct), backend import, `tsc` 0. |
| 2026-07-11 | **Per-unit rates (₹/MT · ₹/CFT · ₹/CBM · ₹/Brass) with operator unit choice.** The same product can be priced by several units, for BOTH the product default AND per-customer, and the weighbridge operator picks the billing unit per truck — the system then auto-selects that unit's rate + quantity. **Non-breaking / additive** (existing single-rate tenants unchanged: legacy `default_rate` + `unit=NULL` party rates become the product's base-unit rate; a token with no `billing_unit` bills exactly as before). **Data:** new `product_unit_rates(company_id, product_id, unit, rate)` UNIQUE(product_id, unit) = per-unit default rates; `party_rates.unit` (nullable) = per-unit customer rate; `tokens.billing_unit` (nullable) = operator's chosen unit. **New `services/pricing.py` — the single resolver** replacing the 5 duplicated `party→product→0` ladders + the kg `_div` maps: `resolve_rate(db, party, product, unit)` (customer-unit → customer-legacy(base) → product-unit → product.default_rate(base) → 0) + `token_quantity(token, unit, product)` (VOLUME unit from `volume_cft`: CFT as-is · CBM ÷35.3147 · Brass ÷100; WEIGHT unit from `net_weight` kg: MT ÷1000 · Qtl ÷100 · KG raw) + `validate_billing_unit` (**"block it"**: a weighed/bridge truck can't bill in a volume unit — raised at token create). Wired into `_auto_create_invoice` (uses `token.billing_unit or product.unit`), `print_token`, and the manual `create_invoice` auto-fill (now unit-aware — finally applies customer rates on manual invoices). **Endpoints:** `GET/PUT /api/v1/products/unit-rates` (per-unit default matrix; PUT mirrors the base-unit cell into `products.default_rate`; defined before `/products/{id}`); `parties` `rates/matrix` cells + `rates/bulk` body + `effective-rate` all gained `unit`; new `GET/PUT /api/v1/app-settings/rate-units` (which unit columns show; default `MT,CFT,CUM,BRASS`). **Frontend:** `PricingMatrixPage` grids now have **one ₹ column per rate-unit** (Default Rates + Customer/Supplier); `TokenPageV1` gains a **"Bill in"** unit picker (weight units always; volume units only in Volume method; blank = product unit); `InvoicesPage` product-select + line Unit dropdown now call `effective-rate?unit=` to fill the matching per-unit rate. Verified: real-DB rollback test 16/16 (ladder incl. party-CFT-override + legacy-NULL→base; CFT/CBM/Brass/MT quantities; 100 CFT × ₹42 = ₹4,200; weighbridge+CFT blocked 400), backend import, 46 Tally tests, `tsc` 0. ⚠ **Billing-core change** — deploy after the current stack is confirmed live + verify on one tenant. |
| 2026-07-11 | **Dedicated Advances / Prepaid page.** A focused screen to record and track customer prepayments (advances) and supplier prepayments, instead of doing it via the general Payments screen. New `AdvancesPage` (Accounts hub **"Advances"** tab + `/advances` route): a **Customer ⇄ Supplier** toggle, a KPI strip (total advances Cr + count of parties holding one), a **"Record Advance"** dialog (party picker + amount + date + mode + reference/notes), and a `<DataTable>` of every party with a current advance-on-account (advance Cr + net Dr/Cr, per-row "+ Add", CSV). **Zero backend change** — an advance is just a payment with no invoice allocation: the dialog POSTs `/api/v1/payments/receipts` (customer) or `/payments/vouchers` (supplier) with `allocations: []`; the balances table reads `/api/v1/reports/party-balances?party_type=…` (advance column). Fully-consumed advances (auto-applied to a bill) drop off the list. Verified: `tsc` 0 errors. (Relies on Feature A's advance-aware balances — deploys together.) |
| 2026-07-11 | **Pricing → Default Rates bulk editor.** Editing each product's default rate one-by-one on the Products page was slow; added a bulk editor so all products' `default_rate` (+ GST %) are set on one screen. `PricingMatrixPage` restructured into **3 top tabs** — **Default Rates** · **Customer Rates** · **Supplier Rates** (the old per-party matrix moved under Customer/Supplier; `mode` now derives from the tab, the old inline customer/supplier toggle removed). New `DefaultRatesEditor` (product · HSN · unit · editable Default Rate + GST %, dirty-row highlight, search, CSV) with a **"Set all shown to ₹X"** helper (fills every filtered row) — dirty rows only are saved. Backend: new `PUT /api/v1/products/default-rates` (`ProductRatesBulkRequest{items:[{product_id,default_rate?,gst_rate?}]}`, admin/operator, company-scoped, unknown/foreign ids skipped, negatives rejected) — **defined before `PUT /products/{id}`** so the literal path isn't captured as a `{product_id}` UUID. Verified: route registered + ordered correctly, real-DB rollback test (updates default_rate 500→777.77 + gst 5→12, unknown id skipped), backend import, `tsc` 0 errors. |
| 2026-07-11 | **Agents (brokers/dalals) + commission report card (Feature B).** Associate an **agent** to a token / invoice / gate pass and pay each agent by commission. **Additive/non-breaking** (mirrors the Driver/Transporter master + nullable-FK pattern). New `agents` master (`models/agent.py`, `routers/agents.py` — `GET/POST/PUT /api/v1/agents`, no DELETE → `is_active`) with per-agent commission config: `commission_type` (`per_mt` · `pct_of_taxable` · `pct_of_grand_total` · `flat_per_invoice`) + `commission_rate`. New nullable `agent_id` FK on **tokens · invoices · gate_passes** (DDL `ALTER … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, after the `agents` CREATE in `get_runtime_ddl`) + `invoices.commission_amount` snapshot. Stamped on `create_token`/`create_volume_token`, **auto-carried token→invoice** in `_auto_create_invoice` (`agent_id=token.agent_id`), settable on manual `create_invoice`, and on the gate-pass raw INSERT. **Commission snapshotted at finalise** (`finalise_invoice` → `services/commission.py::compute_commission(agent, invoice)`; `per_mt` uses `net_weight_kg/1000`) so the report card is a stable SUM immune to later rate changes; only for `COMMISSION_INVOICE_TYPES=('sale',)` (widen to include purchase = one constant). New `agent_commission_payments` table + `POST/GET /agents/{id}/payouts`. **Report card:** `GET /agents/{id}/report?date_from&date_to` → `{earned=Σ commission_amount, paid=Σ payouts, due, invoice_count, total_sale_value, invoices[], payouts[]}` + `GET /agents/report-summary` (all agents). Frontend: `AgentsPage` (`/agents`, master DataTable merged with earned/paid/due + create/edit dialog) · `AgentReportPage` (`/agents/:id`, 4 KPI cards + invoice drilldown DataTable + "Record payout" dialog + payouts list) · sidebar **"Agents"** (Handshake) in COMMERCIAL · agent picker on the weighbridge token form (shown only when agents exist). Verified: real-DB rollback test 11/11 (compute_commission all 4 bases; per_mt ₹20/MT × 10 MT = ₹200 snapshot; report earned 300 / paid 100 / due 200 / count 2 / total ₹10 500; summary foots), backend import, 46 Tally tests, `tsc` 0 errors. Deferred nicety: agent picker on the manual InvoicesPage create dialog (backend already accepts `agent_id`; the token→invoice auto-carry covers the weighbridge flow). |
| 2026-07-11 | **Customer/Supplier advances + advance-aware balances (Feature A).** A party pays money up front and it's **exhausted as they take material**, plus a balance-by-party view for customers AND suppliers. **Root insight:** an advance was already *recordable* — a `PaymentReceipt`/`PaymentVoucher` with no (or partial) invoice allocation leaves an unallocated remainder (`amount − Σ InvoicePayment.amount`); `_validate_allocations` only caps `Σalloc ≤ amount` — but it was **invisible** because `services/balances.py::recompute_party_balance` read only the Invoice table, so `current_balance` ignored it (while the party-ledger `closing_balance` *did* post it → the two diverged exactly on advances). **A1 (fix):** new `party_advance_remaining(db, party_id)` + `recompute_party_balance` now nets `− Σ unallocated receipt remainder` (customer credit) and `+ Σ unallocated voucher remainder` (supplier prepayment) into `current_balance`; CustomerPicker/Parties/360-header already render signed Dr/Cr so they "just work". Proven invariant: applying an advance moves money from receipt-remainder into `Invoice.amount_paid`, so net balance is **unchanged** (no double count). Safe rollout (recomputes per-party-on-event; admin `POST /api/v1/parties/recompute-balances` backfills all). **A3 (auto-exhaust):** `invoices.py::_apply_party_advances(db, inv)` (FIFO) runs inside `finalise_invoice` — sale consumes customer receipts, purchase consumes supplier vouchers, reusing `InvoicePayment` + `_settle_invoice`; gated by app-setting `advance.auto_apply` (default ON). Manual `POST /api/v1/invoices/{id}/apply-advance` + a **Wallet** button on finalised non-paid rows. **A4:** `Party360Stats.advance_balance` + an "Advance on Account / Advance Paid" KPI card on `CustomerProfilePage`. **A5:** new `GET /api/v1/reports/party-balances?party_type=all\|customer\|supplier` (bills / advance / net computed from source, correct even if a stored `current_balance` is stale) → new `PartyBalancesPage` (`<DataTable>` Party·Type·Bills·Advance·Net + CSV, customer/supplier chips) mounted as Accounts-hub **"Balances"** tab + `/party-balances` route. **A2:** Payments dialog shows a live "₹X will be recorded as an advance (on account)" hint when amount exceeds allocations. Non-breaking: no schema change (advances ride existing receipts/vouchers + `invoice_payments`); only behavioural change is advances now moving `current_balance` (a correctness fix). Verified: real-DB rollback test (₹10k advance → −10 000 → finalise ₹6k sale → auto-applied, invoice paid, −4 000, advance left 4 000; 9/9), 43/43 Tally tests, `tsc` 0 errors. (Pre-existing unrelated: `tests/test_accounting_audit.py` mock lacks `write_off_amount` → its `_validate_allocations` case errors on clean HEAD too — not touched here.) |
| 2026-07-08 | **Split a sale invoice into Cash (non-GST) + UPI (GST) bills.** For the "customer paid part cash / part UPI, client wants TWO invoices" requirement. **First, the zero-code answer:** a single invoice can already take multiple payment receipts of different modes (`invoice_payments` has no per-invoice uniqueness; `_settle_invoice` sums them) — record a `cash` receipt + a `upi` receipt against the same bill (Record Payment ×2) and it settles `paid`; this is the GST-clean "one supply = one tax invoice" path. **New feature for the explicit 2-invoice ask:** `POST /api/v1/invoices/{id}/split` (`InvoiceSplitRequest{parts:[{tax_type,ratio,payment_mode?}]}`, exactly 2 parts, ratios sum 1.0) splits ONE **draft sale** invoice into two child drafts **by quantity** — each line's qty is apportioned by the part ratios (first part rounded 3dp, second = exact remainder so qty re-sums), same rate on both, freight + flat discount apportioned by ratio (percentage discount + TCS scale automatically), and GST charged **only on the GST part**. The cash part gets `tax_type='non_gst'` → **Bill of Supply** (CINV series, excluded from GSTR-1/Tally); the UPI part `tax_type='gst'` → **Tax Invoice** (INV series). New `_create_split_child()` reuses `gst_service.calculate_invoice_totals` + the exact clone shape of `issue_note` and sets `tax_type` **explicitly** — this path intentionally bypasses the party `default_payment_mode`→tax_type lock (the only place a saved cash-party can also emit a GST bill). Both children keep the source `token_id`; the source draft is deleted (items then row, `move_to_supplement` pattern). Numbering/finalise/Tally/GSTR-1 need no change — they already key off `tax_type` (CINV `cash_sale_invoice` sequence pre-existed). Frontend: a **Scissors** action on draft **sale** rows (gated by `canEditDraft`, not on purchase) opens `SplitInvoiceDialog` — a Cash%↔UPI% slider with a live rupee preview of each resulting bill (cash = base only; UPI = taxable + GST + total, computed client-side from line qty×rate×gst_rate; server is authoritative). Operator then finalises each child and records the matching receipt. Verified: split math foots against real `gst_service` (cash ₹2,000 no-GST + GST ₹3,000+₹150; qty 4+6=10 MT; taxable re-sums ₹5,000), route registered (POST …/split → 201), backend import OK, 43/43 Tally tests, `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0 errors, frontend serves clean. **Compliance note:** splitting one physical supply across a Tax Invoice + a Bill of Supply is a common Indian dual-billing pattern but is a tax decision for the client's CA — the feature enables it, it doesn't judge it. |

DashboardPage `Number(t.first_weight) / 1000`; PrivateAdminPage column header "Net Wt (MT)"; TokenPage CSV columns relabelled; TokenPageV1 + TokenDetailModal `wFmt` returns `(kg/1000).toFixed(3) + ' MT'`; CameraScalePage shows MT large + kg small; production cycle form rebuilt in MT — `fmtKg` → `fmtMt`, state in MT, `mtToKg()` only at save boundary, `kgToMtStr` on edit-load, labels "Input (MT)" / "Output (MT)"; `utils/invoice_diff.py` labels + values converted to MT (3dp). | 2026-06-01 | **Raw material tracking** on production cycles. New columns `products.is_raw_material BOOLEAN` + `production_cycles.raw_material_id UUID → products.id`. ProductCreate/Update payloads accept the flag; ProductsPage adds "Raw material?" toggle. Production cycle entry shows raw-material picker (filtered to `is_raw_material=true`). On finalise, `post_cycle_input()` posts a NEGATIVE `cycle_input` movement against the raw material (consumption) and `post_cycle_outputs()` posts POSITIVE `cycle_output` movements for each finished product — closes the loop: purchase invoice → raw stock up · cycle finalise → raw stock down + finished stock up · sale invoice → finished stock down. |

| 2026-06-02 | **Pydantic Decimal → JSON string** gotcha fix. Pydantic V2 serialises `Decimal` as a JSON string, but TypeScript types declared `number`, causing `TypeError: t.toFixed is not a function` (white screen on CustomerProfilePage). Convention now documented in CLAUDE.md → Shared UI Components: **ALWAYS** `Number(value ?? 0).toFixed(...)`. CustomerProfilePage rewritten with defensive `Number()` coercion + `?? []` on every list. |
| 2026-06-02 | **Payment-mode drives invoice tax_type + Tally gating**. New `parties.default_payment_mode VARCHAR(20)` (`'cash'` | `'online'`). On invoice create, `effective_tax_type` is derived: `cash` → `non_gst` (Bill of Supply); `online` → respects payload `tax_type` (defaults GST). Tally sync now returns 400 if `invoice.tax_type != 'gst'`; bulk-sync filter adds `Invoice.tax_type == 'gst'`; `/tally/pending` excludes non-GST. New `GET /api/v1/reports/gst-split` returns summary + monthly + top-cash-customers; new `GstSplitReportPage` at `/reports?tab=gst-split` (KPI cards + stacked monthly bar + per-month table + top cash customers). Parties form adds "Mode of Payment" select. |
| 2026-06-02 | `parties.default_payment_mode` default flipped from `'online'` → `'cash'`. Rationale: typical stone-crusher SME walk-in customers are cash-based and only need a Bill of Supply; GST customers are the exception and flagged explicitly. Schema default, model default, and DDL `ALTER COLUMN ... SET DEFAULT 'cash'` all updated. |
| 2026-06-06 | **CFT-only volume system** (BREAKING storage-unit change). m³ removed from the entire app; CFT is now the canonical volume unit and kg/CFT the canonical density unit — matches Indian stone-crusher industry practice. (a) Schema: `tokens.volume_m3` renamed → `volume_cft NUMERIC(10,3)`; `products.bulk_density` semantics changed from t/m³ to kg/CFT (column name preserved). (b) One-shot data migration via guarded DO block in `ddl.py` — marker `app_settings.units_migrated_to_cft_v1` prevents re-running; on existing tenants: `UPDATE tokens SET volume_cft = volume_m3 × 35.3147` + `DROP COLUMN volume_m3` + `UPDATE products SET bulk_density = bulk_density × 28.3168`. Idempotent — new tenants no-op past the marker insert. (c) Calculation: `weight_kg = volume_cft × bulk_density(kg/CFT)` (was `m3 × t/m3 × 1000`). Round-trip drift < 0.001 % on real data. (d) Backend: Token model + schemas + `POST /tokens/volume` payload field renamed; PDF templates (`token_a4.html` + `token_thermal.html`) rewritten to show CFT and kg/CFT only — no m³ display anywhere. (e) Frontend: ProductsPage label "Bulk Density (kg/CFT)" + typical values (aggregate 42.5, sand 48.1, GSB 53.8, stone dust 43.9) + tip showing t/m³ × 28.32 conversion; TokenPageV1 m³/ft³ unit selector removed, `TYRE_VOLUME_M3` → `TYRE_VOLUME_CFT` (106/247/353/459/600), `volumeM3` → `volumeCft`, dualFmt CFT formula simplified to `kg ÷ kg_per_cft`; OperatorKioskPage tyre tiles show CFT, voice prompt says "cubic feet"; types/index.ts `Token.volume_m3` → `volume_cft`. (f) Seed script (`seed_tenant_demo.py`) bulk densities + volume tokens updated to kg/CFT + CFT. (g) eInvoice NIC unit map already mapped `cft` → `CFT`, no change. |
| 2026-06-16 | **Production → inventory now auto-posts on save** (was finalise-only). Creating a production cycle immediately credits finished-goods **Stock on Hand** (`product_stock`) and debits the raw material — no separate "Finalise" click. `create_cycle` sets `is_finalised=True` + calls new `_post_cycle_stock()`. Cycles are now **editable + deletable** with stock kept correct: `update_cycle` reverses the OLD postings (new `product_stock.reverse_cycle_stock()` — `cycle_output_cancelled` / `cycle_input_cancelled` movements) then re-posts the NEW values (net delta = new−old); `delete_cycle` reverses postings before deleting (no orphaned stock). Frontend ProductionPage: Edit/Delete always available (re-sync/reverse stock), messaging updated ("finished goods added to Stock on Hand"). Legacy draft cycles keep the Finalise button. |
| 2026-06-16 | **Horizon 3 (partial) — scaling + ITC** (multi-branch foundation + GSTR-2B; fraud + barrier deferred to a follow-up). **(A) Full multi-branch foundation (backward-compatible):** new `branches` table + Branch CRUD (`/api/v1/branches`, admin) + `GET /branches/current`. `branch_id` (NULLABLE → existing rows = implicit default branch, so current numbering/queries untouched) added to tokens, invoices, payment_receipts/vouchers, product_stock, production_cycles, users, number_sequences. Branch context = `get_current_branch_id` dependency (admins switch via `X-Branch-Id` header sent by the UI picker; others pinned to `user.branch_id`; None = all/default). **Per-branch numbering** — `next_gate_pass_no`/`next_doc_no`/`_next_invoice_no` take a `branch_id` and filter NULL-safely (`branch_id == None` → `IS NULL`), so each branch gets its own gap-free invoice/GP series and the default branch keeps its existing sequences (token_no is random per company-day → unchanged). `create_token`/`create_volume_token`/`create_invoice` stamp branch; token + invoice lists filter by active branch. Frontend: `BranchPicker` header dropdown + `BranchAdminPage` (`/admin/branches`) + sidebar entry + `api.ts` forwards `X-Branch-Id`. **DEFERRED:** per-branch *stock* (product_stock.product_id is globally UNIQUE — needs a constraint rework + `_get_or_create_stock_row` rewrite, not shipped untested); reports/dashboards not yet branch-scoped beyond token/invoice lists. ⚠️ per-branch numbering touches the gap-free invariant — verify on deploy. **(B) GSTR-2B ITC reconciliation:** new `POST /api/v1/reports/gstr2b-reconcile` (multipart 2B JSON upload, stateless — no writes). Parses `data.docdata.b2b` (tolerant of layout/field-name variants), matches against finalised purchase invoices by GSTIN + normalised invoice no (±₹1 tol) → matched / value-mismatch / in-2B-not-books (ITC available, enter these) / in-books-not-2B (ITC at risk, chase supplier) + ITC summary. `Gstr2bReconcilePage` (upload + 4 KPI cards + 4 buckets) as a Reports-hub "GSTR-2B (ITC)" tab. |
| 2026-06-16 | **Horizon 2 — differentiation pack** (3 features, BCG gap review). **(A) Royalty / mining transit-pass:** new `royalty_passes` + `royalty_pass_consumptions` tables. Router `/api/v1/royalty` — CRUD passes; `POST /passes/{id}/consume` (records a draw-down, auto-exhausts at zero balance, overrun flagged); `GET /reconciliation` (authorised vs consumed vs completed-purchase-token inbound MT + "unaccounted" delta); `GET /alerts` (expiring within N days). `RoyaltyPassesPage` (reconciliation KPIs + utilisation bars + create/consume dialogs) → Materials hub "Royalty Passes" tab + `/royalty`. The killer stone-crusher differentiator. **(B) Customer portal + static UPI:** new `customer_users` table — a SEPARATE auth realm; JWTs carry `scope='customer'` + party_id so a portal token can't hit staff endpoints and a customer only sees their own party. `routers/portal.py` — dual-mode login (tenant from subdomain), `/me` (balance net of notes), `/invoices`, `/invoices/{id}/pdf` (ownership-enforced), `/statement`, `/payment-info`, `/change-password`; standalone bearer dep (not staff OAuth2). Admin mgmt on parties: `POST/GET/DELETE /parties/{id}/portal-account` + reset-password. Static UPI: `app-settings /upi-config` (vpa+payee+enabled) shown on the portal Pay tab as VPA + `upi://` deep link (no live gateway — manual reconciliation, per locked decision). Frontend: standalone `CustomerPortalPage` at `/portal` (own login + dashboard), `PortalAccessDialog` on Customer-360, Settings "UPI / Portal" tab. **(C) Offline resilience:** `lib/offlineQueue.ts` — token POSTs that fail on a network error (or while offline) are stored in localStorage and replayed on reconnect (+60s safety retry); server numbering stays gap-free (assigned at sync). TokenPageV1 weighbridge create queues offline ("Saved offline — will sync…"); `OfflineIndicator` header pill (Offline / N pending). PWA: `manifest.webmanifest` + `sw.js` app-shell SW (network-first nav → cached shell offline; `/api/*` never cached), registered in `main.tsx` (prod only). Agent: scale streams live (non-transactional) weight so it's NOT buffered (replaying stale weights would be wrong) — it already degrades gracefully + serves weight on local `:9002`; added a `cloud_online` status flag for observability. |

| 2026-06-12 | **Gate pass fully wired across the app** (previously only the ANPR happy-path issued one). (a) New shared helper `app/services/numbering.py::next_gate_pass_no()` — single gap-free, row-locked (`WITH FOR UPDATE`) allocator for `GP/25-26/NNNN`; `anpr.py::_next_gate_pass_no` now delegates to it so ANPR and manual paths can't diverge. (b) **Manual token creation now auto-issues a gate pass at entry** — both `create_token` (weighbridge, status OPEN) and `create_volume_token` allocate `gate_pass_no` via the shared helper, mirroring the ANPR auto-entry behaviour. So every truck gets a gate pass whether ANPR is on or off. (c) `TokenResponse` schema now exposes `gate_pass_no`, `source`, `anpr_entry_at`, `anpr_exit_at` (previously dropped — the auto gate pass was invisible to `GET /tokens`). (d) PDF slips (`token_a4.html` + `token_thermal.html`) print `gate_pass_no` (falling back to legacy free-text `gate_pass`) — the printed slip is now the physical gate pass. (e) Frontend: `Token` type gains the 4 fields; TokenPageV1 shows the GP number under the token # on each row + a Gate Pass CSV column; TokenDetailModal adds a Gate Pass card + gate-movement (in/out) panel. The legacy free-text `tokens.gate_pass` field is retained as an optional note. (f) **Accepted design tradeoff (code-reviewed):** the GP number is gap-FREE at allocation (no two trucks share one) but NOT gap-less over time — issued at gate ENTRY (token create), so a cancelled/abandoned token leaves a hole that maps to a real arrival event and is never reclaimed. This is intentional and differs from `token_no` (assigned at COMPLETED) / `invoice_no` (assigned at FINALISE). |
| 2026-06-11 | **CI/CD auto-deploy pipeline** (`.github/workflows/deploy.yml` + `scripts/ci-deploy.sh`). Every push to `main` SSHes into the Hostinger VPS and runs the deploy script: `git pull`, detect what changed, rebuild only the affected side (frontend `npm ci && npm run build` → nginx docroot, backend `pip install` if requirements changed + `systemctl restart weighbridge`), `nginx -t && systemctl reload nginx`, `curl /api/v1/health` rollback signal, append to `/var/log/weighbridge-deploy.log`. Workflow then purges Cloudflare cache (zone + token from repo secrets) and pings the live URL for sanity. `concurrency: deploy-vps` prevents overlapping runs. `paths-ignore` on `**/*.md`, `docs/**`, `plans/**` so doc-only commits don't deploy. `workflow_dispatch` for manual runs. Setup guide at `docs/CI_CD_SETUP.md` covers SSH-key generation on VPS, the 4 required GitHub secrets (`VPS_HOST`, `VPS_USER`, `VPS_SSH_KEY`, `VPS_PORT`) + 2 optional CF secrets, troubleshooting, and rollback procedure. Replaces the manual `ssh + git pull + npm run build + cp + reload` runbook that left 4+ commits un-deployed for a week. |

Each detector runs in an isolated try/except — one failure never blocks others. Returns `{overall: high/medium/low/ok, detectors: {...}}`. Thresholds configurable via `GET/PUT /api/v1/reports/anomaly-config` (stored in `app_settings.anomaly_config`). | 2026-06-20 | **CRITICAL fix: per-statement DDL bootstrap.** `get_column_migrations()` listed `ALTER TABLE production_cycles ADD COLUMN raw_material_id` **before** the `CREATE TABLE production_cycles` in the same list. On any DB lacking that table, the ALTER raised "relation does not exist"; because `main.py::_apply_all_ddl` ran the WHOLE list in ONE transaction, that error aborted the transaction and the commit rolled back — so **every** statement after it silently never applied: `production_cycles`, `product_stock`, `branches`, `royalty_passes`, `delivery_challans`, `gate_passes`, `gate_pass_daily_seq`, `gate_camera_events`, `anpr_events`, `customer_users`. Result: Gate Register, Production, Product Inventory, Branches, Royalty, Challans, Credit/Debit Notes, ANPR, Customer Portal & GSTR-2B all 500'd on a missing relation (surfaced as the generic "Something went wrong. Try again."). Fix: `_apply_all_ddl` (and the multi-tenant `router.py` provisioner) now run **each** DDL statement in its **own** transaction — one failure is logged & skipped, all others apply; idempotent stmts self-heal next start. Also moved the `raw_material_id` ALTER after its CREATE so it applies on a fresh DB in one pass. Verified: all 10 missing tables create, 0 residual failures, gate-pass INSERT succeeds, no other ALTER-before-CREATE traps. Tables auto-create on the next backend restart (CI deploy). |
| 2026-06-20 | **Accounting-calculation audit + fixes** (senior-partner review; `backend/tests/test_accounting_audit.py`, 41/41 pass ×3). **(1) Volume-billing density-unit trap (CRITICAL):** Products density labels/hints (en+hi) said "kg/m³ (~1500)" while the backend stores & bills in **kg/CFT (~42.5)** — an operator following the old hint would over-bill every volume token ~35×. Relabelled to kg/CFT with correct typical values (aggregate 42.5, sand 48.1, GSB 53.8, stone dust 43.9) + "per cubic FOOT" warning. **(2) Grand-total round-off (HIGH):** `gst_service.calculate_invoice_totals` now rounds grand_total to the **nearest whole rupee** (standard Indian tax invoice); the previously-dead `round_off` column now carries the ± adjustment so the invoice still foots (`taxable+cgst+sgst+igst+freight+tcs+round_off==grand_total`). **(3) GSTR-1 excludes non-GST (HIGH/compliance):** added `tax_type='gst'` to GSTR-1 summary + JSON B2B/B2C/HSN selects so Bill-of-Supply sales no longer inflate GSTR-1 turnover (GSTR-3B already filtered). **(4) P&L on taxable basis (HIGH):** `/reports/profit-loss` revenue & COGS now use `taxable_amount` (ex-GST) not `grand_total`, and revenue is net of credit/debit notes. **(5) `dualFmt` display:** treated kg/CFT as kg/m³ — rewritten CFT-correct. **Verified-correct (no change):** Decimal-everywhere money, per-line GST on post-discount base, CGST/SGST never loses a paisa, non-GST→0 tax on all create paths, `amount_due=grand_total` at draft create on both manual+token paths, volume formula `volume_cft × bulk_density(kg/CFT)`, CFT migration constants. **Flagged for follow-up (not auto-fixed):** GSTR-3B 3.1(a) + GSTR-1-JSON CDNR note-netting; payment over-allocation guard; `party.current_balance` only half-maintained; reports not company-scoped (latent under per-tenant-DB). |

The Settings → Tally "Auto-sync when invoice is finalised" toggle (already in the UI) is now wired to real behaviour. | 2026-06-25 | **Fix: PWA icon not showing correctly (root cause + fix).** The installed/home-screen icon rendered clipped, washed-out, or with dark corner halos. Root cause: the icon PNGs had **transparent rounded corners** but were declared `"purpose": "any maskable"` in the manifest — a maskable icon must be full-bleed (reach every edge), and the green accent bar (bottom edge) + green dot (top-right corner) sat **outside the maskable 80% safe zone**, so Android's circle/squircle mask clipped them and the transparent corners showed through; `apple-touch-icon` transparency also produced black corner halos on iOS. Fix: regenerated all icons **full-bleed solid navy (opaque to every edge, no transparent corners)** via `scripts/gen_pwa_icons.py`; split into **separate `any` and `maskable`** manifest entries (maskable variants keep the WB wordmark + underline inside the inner safe zone with ~24% margin); `apple-touch-icon` now fully opaque. Shipped under **new `-v3` filenames** (`icon-192-v3.png`, `icon-512-v3.png`, `icon-maskable-192-v3.png`, `icon-maskable-512-v3.png`, `apple-touch-icon-v3.png`, `favicon-32-v3.png`) to bust every HTTP / Cloudflare / service-worker cache; old broken PNGs deleted; `manifest.webmanifest` + `index.html` repointed. Note: already-installed PWAs capture the icon at install time — users must remove + re-add to home screen to pick up the new icon. |
