| 2026-06-26 | **Live weight not reaching the browser (weighment dialog stuck "OFFLINE") — root-caused: multi-worker WebSocket split.** The scale agent's Discovery UI showed SCALE CONNECTED + CLOUD ONLINE + rising push count, yet the dialog's SCALE READING stayed **OFFLINE**. Proven cause (from `scripts/deploy-vps.sh`, not assumed): prod ran `uvicorn … --workers 2`, but the live-weight pub/sub (`weight.py::_tenant_weight_managers`) is an **in-memory module-level dict — one copy per worker process**. The agent's `POST /api/v1/weight/external-reading` and the browser's `/ws/weight` WebSocket get load-balanced to **different workers**, so `_broadcast()` on worker A never reaches the WS client on worker B — the push succeeds but the browser sees nothing (intermittent ~50%, depending which worker the WS lands on). **Fix: pin `--workers 1`** in all provisioning (`deploy-vps.sh` systemd unit + `install-services.ps1` + `fix-services.ps1`, each with a guard comment) — one process co-locates the push and the WebSocket; async FastAPI on 1 worker is ample for weighbridge concurrency. **To scale past 1 worker you MUST first add a cross-process bus (Redis pub/sub or Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY) for the weight channel.** Live prod is fixed by editing the running unit (CI does not re-run deploy-vps.sh): `sudo sed -i 's/--workers 2/--workers 1/' /etc/systemd/system/weighbridge.service && sudo systemctl daemon-reload && sudo systemctl restart weighbridge`. |
| 2026-06-26 | **Weight STILL offline after `--workers 1` — 2nd root cause: agent posting to the apex (301 drops the POST) + closed the WebSocket auth gap.** The server's own `GET /api/v1/weight/ping?tenant=<slug>` proved it: `manager:active` but `scale_connected:false` (no readings landed). The agent's `cloud_url` was the apex `https://weighbridgesetu.com`, which **301-redirects to www**; Python `requests` follows a 301 by converting POST→GET and **dropping the body**, so the reading never reached the backend (the agent still saw a 2xx and counted it "pushed"). A POST to the tenant **subdomain** returns 200 and ping immediately flips to `scale_connected:true, weight_kg:…`. **Fixes:** (1) repointed the live agent's `cloud_url` to `https://megna-trading.weighbridgesetu.com` + restarted (verified pushing). (2) Robust agent code — new `scale_agent.py::_effective_push_base(cloud_url, tenant_slug)` auto-routes the apex/`www` host to `https://<tenant_slug>.weighbridgesetu.com` (custom domains / localhost / already-subdomained hosts untouched), so no future install hits the redirect trap even with the default apex `cloud_url`. (3) **Closed the WebSocket auth gap:** in multi-tenant mode `/ws/weight` now REQUIRES `?token=<JWT>` whose `tenant` claim equals `?tenant=` (closes with 4003 on missing/invalid/mismatched), so one tenant can't even READ another's live feed; `useWeight.ts` now sends the JWT (new `getAuthToken()` in `useAuth.ts`). Single-tenant/LAN installs stay open (no token). **⚠ After this deploy, already-open weighbridge tabs must be hard-refreshed once** to load the token-sending frontend (else their WS gets 4003). Verified: agent+backend py_compile + import, `_effective_push_base` unit check (apex→subdomain, localhost untouched), tsc 0 errors. |
| 2026-06-26 | **CRITICAL: `/ws/weight` 500'd on EVERY connect — wrong JWT lib in the auth I just added.** After the worker + agent fixes the browser STILL showed OFFLINE. Proven by a raw WS-handshake probe against prod (`curl -H "Upgrade: websocket" …`) → **HTTP 500**, not `101 Switching Protocols`, so no browser could ever open the socket (this masked the agent + worker fixes — the relay was fine, the handshake itself crashed). Reproduced on the local backend and read the traceback: `weight.py:63 ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'jwt'`. The WS-auth I added did `import jwt` (PyJWT), but **this app uses python-jose (`from jose import jwt`)** — PyJWT isn't installed — and the import was OUTSIDE the try/except, so it 500'd the whole handshake. Fix: `from jose import jwt as _jwt` (matches `dependencies.py`); dropped the `reason=` kwarg from the reject `close()` calls (use bare `close(code=4403)`/`4001`) to avoid any cross-version `WebSocket.close()` signature issue. **Verified locally with raw handshakes:** no-token → 403, valid token + matching `tenant` claim → 101 (accept), valid token + mismatched tenant → 403. nginx WS proxy (`location /ws/` + Upgrade headers) confirmed correct. **Lesson:** when adding a lib import, match the one the codebase already uses (`grep` it first) — and always test the actual handshake (`101` vs `403` vs `500`), not just the server-side push state. |

| 2026-06-26 | **Tally response-parser hardening — a silent "imported 0" no longer reads as success (client review finding).** A client's Tally **Import Configuration** screenshot showed **"Overwrite voucher when a voucher with same GUID exists" = No**. With that setting a re-sent voucher (a retry, or a re-synced *corrected* invoice) is **skipped as a duplicate exception** — Tally returns `CREATED=0 ALTERED=0 EXCEPTIONS=1` with **no `LINEERROR`** — but `_parse_tally_response` (in both `integrations/tally/client.py` and the connector's copy in `agents/tally_connector.py`) fell through to `return True, "Sent to Tally successfully"`, so we'd flip `tally_synced=True` on a voucher that never imported (silent divergence). **Fix:** parse the `<CREATED>/<ALTERED>/<EXCEPTIONS>/<ERRORS>` counts — `CREATED/ALTERED>0` → success; `EXCEPTIONS/ERRORS>0` OR count tags present but all zero → **failure** with an actionable hint (points at the GUID-overwrite setting / a missing master); a **count-less envelope** (older Tally builds) stays leniently successful so we don't false-fail those. Failures now surface in the relay dead-letter list instead of masquerading as synced, and self-heal once overwrite is set to Yes (the retry ALTERs). **DPD runbook:** new `DPD-TALLY-CONNECTOR.md` §0a "Tally Import Configuration (CRITICAL)" requires **Overwrite same-GUID voucher = Yes** + **Record Exceptions and Import**, with an RDP/`\\TSCLIENT` host-resolution note. **Verified:** full app import; 8-case parser unit on BOTH copies (created/altered→ok · lineerror/dup-skip/errors/zero-counts→fail · bare-envelope/non-xml→ok); connector py_compile; 44/44 Tally tests green. | 2026-06-27 | **Live weight reactivity — ~1 s instead of ~30 s (3 fixes across the pipeline).** Operators reported the on-screen weight lagging the physical indicator by up to 30 s. Root-caused end-to-end (probed the live server's `/weight/ping` — single worker, agent pushing, value just frozen at the empty-bridge reading `001.665`, so it was latency, not parse/calibration). **(1) WS reconnect (`useWeight.ts`):** the reconnect backoff capped at **30 s** (`3→6→12→24→30`) and there was no half-dead-socket detection — when Cloudflare/nginx drops the socket without a clean close event the browser held a zombie connection until the OS TCP timeout (~30 s). Now: backoff BASE 1 s / cap 4 s (long 15 s only for code 1013 = no scale manager) **+ a silence watchdog** that force-reconnects if no frame arrives for 8 s (the agent pushes every ~500 ms, so 8 s of silence = dead socket) → recovers in ~1–2 s. **(2) Agent push queue (`scale_agent.py`):** the `_push_worker` was a plain FIFO that POSTed readings **one-by-one** and, on overflow, `put_nowait` dropped the **newest** (kept 20 stale) — so a network hiccup made it stream a backlog of old readings while the screen ran seconds behind. Now **latest-wins**: `_enqueue` drops the **oldest** on overflow, and the worker **coalesces** the queue down to the freshest reading before each POST (`while True: get_nowait()`), so the on-screen weight stays ~1 round-trip behind the scale. Plus a keep-alive `requests.Session` (skips a TLS handshake per POST). **(3) Diagnostics:** added the last raw indicator frame to `GET /weight/ping` (weight data only, no secrets) so the exact frame can be read remotely without LAN access — this is how `001.665` was confirmed. ⚠ The agent fix lives in `backend/agents/scale_agent.py` which runs on the **client weighbridge PC** (NOT auto-deployed by CI) — update the agent there + restart the service; the WS fix ships via CI (hard-refresh the tab once). Verified: `tsc` 0, agent `py_compile` OK, backend import OK. |
| 2026-06-27 | **Tally relay: stale invoice jobs self-heal on retry (so a config change reaches already-queued vouchers).** A relay job stores the voucher XML built **at enqueue time**, and the connector's auto-retry replayed that stored XML — so an invoice queued in full-mode (inventory) BEFORE the operator switched ON "Accounting vouchers (no inventory)" kept failing `EXCEPTIONS=1` forever (this company rejects stock-item vouchers), even though a fresh sync would now build the accepted accounting voucher. Proven by claiming the live `INV/25-26/0099` job: `OBJVIEW="Invoice Voucher View"` + `INVENTORYENTRIES` present (all 4 ledgers — Sunvik/Sales/IGST/Round Off — already existed in Tally, so NOT a missing-master issue). **Fix:** extracted `tally_connector._rebuild_invoice_job_xml(db, job)` (rebuilds an invoice/CN/DN job's voucher from the **current** Tally config) and called it from BOTH `requeue_job` (already did this) AND the **`/jobs/{id}/result` handler on a retryable failure** (`status=='pending'`) — so every auto-retry regenerates the voucher from current config. Net: once the operator flips "Accounting vouchers (no inventory)" ON, the stuck full-mode jobs **rebuild into the accounting voucher on their next retry and import** — no manual re-sync needed. Safe (swallows rebuild errors → keeps stored XML; only rewrites when the XML actually changed). Verified: backend import, 44/44 Tally relay+integration tests. |
| 2026-06-27 | **Invoice forms: Tax Type drives GST visibility + B&W Tally-style PDF.** (UI, `InvoicesPage.tsx`, both Create AND Edit dialogs, Sales + Purchase) `isGst = tax_type==='gst'` now gates everything: **Cash / Bill of Supply (non_gst) hides the GST column entirely and charges no GST**; **GST (Tax Invoice) shows the GST% column + a totals breakdown (Taxable + GST + Total)** instead of just the total. Selecting a party auto-sets Tax Type from its `default_payment_mode` (cash→non_gst, online→gst), so cash customers get a Bill of Supply with no GST shown; the non-GST option stays visible whenever it's the active value (manual pick keeps the USB gate). Submit zeros `gst_rate` on non-GST lines. (PDF, `templates/pdf/invoice.html`) rewrote the A4 invoice into a clean **black-and-white Tally-style tax invoice** (was navy-themed + rendered poorly): title is type-aware (TAX INVOICE / BILL OF SUPPLY / PURCHASE INVOICE / CREDIT/DEBIT NOTE), table-only layout (xhtml2pdf-safe — no flex/grid), GST invoices get the Taxable/GST%/CGST-SGST-IGST columns + HSN tax summary while **Bill-of-Supply drops all tax columns**, totals block omits zero CGST/SGST/IGST/discount/freight/round-off, IRN+QR box only when present, bank/signature/declaration honour the `ps` print-settings toggles. Verified: 4 variants (GST+non-GST × sale+purchase) all render to valid `%PDF` via the xhtml2pdf fallback; GST PDFs ~17 KB (with tax cols) vs non-GST ~12.7 KB (without); `tsc -p tsconfig.app.json` 0. |
| 2026-06-27 | **Fix: invoice PDF "Particulars" column blank.** The PDF item row prints `item.description or item.product.name`, but the invoice form never fills `description` AND `InvoiceItem` had **no `product` relationship** (only the `product_id` FK) — so both resolved empty and the product name never showed. Added `InvoiceItem.product = relationship("Product", lazy="noload")` (noload so it never lazy-loads in async) + eager-loaded it in the PDF query (`_load_invoice`: `selectinload(Invoice.items).selectinload(InvoiceItem.product)`). Now the Particulars column falls back to the product name. Fixes existing AND new invoices (no backfill). Verified: rendered HTML shows the product name when the line has no description; explicit description still wins; backend import (mapper config valid) + 43/43 Tally tests. |
| 2026-06-27 | **CRITICAL fix: IRN stamped on non-GST / cash invoices.** `_try_generate_irn` (auto, on finalise) ran the **demo-mode branch BEFORE the B2B-GSTIN check and had no `tax_type` check at all** — so with e-Invoice enabled + `demo_mode` on, EVERY finalised invoice (including a Bill of Supply / cash `non_gst` invoice, and B2C invoices with no party GSTIN) got a (demo) IRN stamped. IRN/e-Invoice applies ONLY to a GST tax invoice issued to a registered (B2B) party. Fix: both the auto path (`_try_generate_irn`) and the manual `POST /{id}/generate-irn` now require `inv.tax_type == 'gst'` **and** a party GSTIN **before** the demo branch (in addition to the existing `is_enabled` + `auto_generate_on_finalize` gates). So no IRN is ever created for cash/non-GST/B2C invoices, and nothing at all unless e-Invoice is explicitly enabled in Settings → eInvoice. (Note for the user: the e-Invoice/IRN toggle is the **eInvoice** Settings tab — separate from the **E-Way Bill** tab.) Verified: gating unit test — GST+GSTIN+enabled+auto → IRN; non_gst, B2C-no-GSTIN, disabled, auto-off → no IRN (5/5). |
| 2026-07-01 | **Gate Camera Live Feed — live frames via existing `camera_agent.py` (no second agent required).** `GateCameraLivePage` was stuck on "Waiting for agent…" even though Camera & Weighing Scale Monitor showed live images from the same physical cameras. **Root cause:** `GateCameraLivePage` polls `GET /api/v1/gate/latest-snapshot/{position}` every 3 s; that endpoint reads files written by `POST /api/v1/gate/push-snapshot/{position}`. Only the never-run `gate_camera_agent.py` pushed to that endpoint; `camera_agent.py` (the only running agent) only served weighbridge cameras. **Fix (3 parts):** (1) **`camera_agent.py` — new `GateLiveFeedPusher` class**: continuously captures from `gate_cameras.entry/exit` URLs (already in `camera_config.json`) every 3 s and POSTs JPEGs to `push-snapshot` using `X-Agent-Key` header (same `agent_key` already in the config — no separate gate agent key required). Falls back to `cameras.front` if a gate URL is empty. Wired into `main()` alongside `GatePassListener`; shown in startup banner. (2) **`gate.py push-snapshot` — multi-tenant + dual auth**: accepts `tenant_slug` form field to route to the correct tenant DB via `get_tenant_session(tenant_slug)`; accepts `X-Agent-Key` (validated via tenant registry — same key as weighbridge `camera_config.json`) in addition to the existing `X-Gate-Agent-Key`. Per-tenant frame files stored at `uploads/gate/latest/{slug}/{position}.jpg` to prevent cross-tenant frame pollution. (3) **`gate.py latest-snapshot` — multi-tenant path resolution**: reads per-tenant latest dir via `_ctx_tenant_slug()` (ContextVar set by TenantMiddleware); returns the correct `/uploads/gate/latest/{slug}/…` URL prefix in the JSON response. **No new config fields, no second agent process, no new service to install.** Restart `WeighbridgeCameraAgent` on the plant LAN PC (`nssm restart WeighbridgeCameraAgent`) — within ~3 s the Gate Camera Live Feed page shows live frames from `192.168.0.223` (entry) and `192.168.0.224` (exit). **Also:** `GateCameraLivePage.tsx` rewritten to match `CameraScalePage.tsx` dark CCTV aesthetic — dark slate-900 background, scan-line overlay, corner markers, emerald (entry) / rose (exit) accents, status states: `● LIVE` / `◑ LAST FRAME` / `◌ CONNECTING` / `✕ NOT CONFIGURED` / `✕ OFFLINE`, fullscreen mode, animated ping dot, bottom status bar `GATE SURVEILLANCE SYSTEM · Agent Push Mode`. |
